# Handoff board

The working state of the project: who is on what, which interfaces the workstreams agreed on, and where each one stopped. `docs/SPEC.md` is the plan; this is the log of executing it. Update your row when you stop, even mid-task.

## How we work (agents read this first)

- **Where things are.** What to work on next, ranked by impact and effort: [`TRIAGE.md`](TRIAGE.md). Every call the maintainer made, with the options and outcomes: [`DECISIONS.md`](DECISIONS.md) (exported from the Decisions page, https://claude.ai/artifact/E8qHQUAKxkGbW6wQ2F2sCX, where new open questions go). The details behind each row stay here (backlog, log) and in `DIVERGES.md`. When you finish a triage row, delete it from TRIAGE.md and log the W id below.
- **Build features first.** Get a pass working end to end on a real GPU (`pnpm check`), commit, move on. Don't sink time into squeezing milliseconds or making a test match an expectation. If a number is off, write it down (row below, or `DIVERGES.md` if it's a divergence) and keep going. Measurement is for the **gates** in SPEC's roadmap and for big decisions, not for every step.
- **Time-box.** If something fights you for more than ~30 minutes of attempts, record what you tried in your row's "stuck on" and move to the next part of your scope.
- **Diverging from a source is fine. Hiding it isn't.** Every knowing difference from FFX, idTech, three, Lagarde, etc. goes in `docs/DIVERGES.md`.
- **Commit after every run** (CLAUDE.md rule 4): what was tried, numbers, verdict, failures included. Explicit paths, never `git add -A`.
- **Stay in your lane.** Each stream owns the paths in its row. Touching a shared file (`packages/*/src/index.ts`, `src/*.ts`, `package.json`, `scripts/run-headless.mjs`) is allowed but keep the edit to adding your exports or your check, so merges stay trivial.
- **Stopping a server:** kill by port (`lsof -ti tcp:<your port> | xargs kill`), never `pkill -f vite`. A pattern kill takes down every other worktree's server too.
- **Ports.** Each worktree runs its examples server on its own port: `REFLECTIONS_PORT=<port> pnpm examples` / `REFLECTIONS_PORT=<port> pnpm check`. 5370 is the maintainer's own server: agents and the lead never use it (the lead runs main-checkout checks on 5389). `pnpm check` reuses any server already on its port, so a wrong port means testing someone else's build. 5310 belongs to another project.

Verdict (docs/research/probe-layouts.md): compressed cube arrays are **not** faster than octa; compression is (BC6H 0.35-0.41x rgba16f when cache-bound, ~1.0x coherent), for both. Coherent lookups: octa 0.97-1.11x cube-hw (0.84-0.88x at 8/px); today's cube2d-warp 1.24-1.49x. Gate: octa **fails** (at equal memory, octa@320, mirror mip p99 19.9 vs warp 12.7 / cube-hw 0.0; r > 0 equal), stays an option. BC6H **fails** 2x p99 on the roughest mips (2.7x at r=1, `fast`), stays off. rgb9e5 is the near-free 4 B option. Phase 2 core path: `cube-hw`. Found: r < 0.2 lookups p99 35-46/255 for every layout (D101). Stuck/pitfalls: Metal render-pass timestamps unusable for lookups (wall clock + ratios used, D104); begin/end-only compute timestamps over >4 prefilters per command buffer lost the device (OOM), bracket with one-texel passes instead. | D1 | Root cause of the 75/255 point-light-highlight nondeterminism in examples/probes | debug | nothing in `src/` (investigation only) | 5387 | time-boxed, cause **not** found, nothing loosened (branch `worktree-agent-a3cd4973edc265424`) | **Reproduced on an idle machine, deterministically enough:** stop the page, `setGrid(true, 0.05)`, then render with `R.render(); R._animation.stop()` into a FloatType `RenderTarget` and read back: frame 0 differs from frames 1+ in ~430 px (1314 floats), frames 1+ are bit-identical; interleaving other GPU work (4 renders of the full scene per frame) flips single later frames (1 in 16 to 24). Same in the canvas: 75-126/255 on the grid spheres' highlights. **Ruled out (each with a run):** (1) three's per-frame caches / D802 class: the flip survives with no frame boundary games, and the **GPU command stream for the flipped frame is identical** (logged `writeBuffer`/`writeTexture`/`createBuffer`/`createBindGroup`/`createRenderPipeline`/`setPipeline`/`setBindGroup`/`setVertexBuffer`/`drawIndexed`: frames 1-3 issue the same pipeline, the same bind groups, the same buffers and no writes at all, yet frame 0 vs 1 differ); CPU uniform buffers of the grid draws are bit-identical every frame (snapshotted at `backend.draw`). (2) Shadows: `shadowMap.enabled = false` or `castShadow = false` still flips (the grid doesn't receive shadows; its bindings are render group, object group and the DFG LUT only). (3) Probes / env: `scene.environmentNode = null` and the legacy TSL lookup both still flip. (4) Our scene: a **minimal scene** (the two grid `InstancedMesh`es + two `PointLight`s, no shadows, no env, nothing from this repo in its draws) flips the same way. (5) Highlight ulp-chaos: at roughness 0.5 the same ~430 px differ by up to 25 % relative (median 0.17 %), so it is a fixed pixel set with a real input difference, not D-term cancellation. (6) Derivatives (`dpdx(normalView)` output), `positionView`, `positionViewDirection`, `normalView` (MeshNormalNodeMaterial) and `lightViewPosition()` as output: bit-stable under the same conditions; a non-instanced sphere with the lit material: stable. (7) Harness port collisions (F2). **Where it is:** the differing pixels sit on the upper part of one row of grid instances next to the warm light (x 208-477, y 187-355 at 960x540, every ~54 px), only with a lit material on an `InstancedMesh`. **Best remaining hypothesis:** below three (Chrome/Dawn on Metal, Apple GPU): the same draw reads different data or runs a different shader variant for some instances, e.g. a hazard on freshly created / mapped-at-creation vertex or uniform buffers, or a driver recompile of the pipeline; it depends on what else ran on the GPU, which is why other worktrees' load triggers it. Scripts: `docs/research/d1/` (`drive.mjs` opens a page in headless Chrome over CDP and evaluates a file; `minimal-scene.js` gives the per-frame diff counts, `command-log.js` the per-frame WebGPU call log). Next step: a standalone three-only page (minimal scene above, render 50x into a FloatType target with readback, optionally a heavy compute dispatch between), then raw WebGPU with the dumped WGSL to drop three; try `--enable-dawn-features=dump_shaders` / `disable_symbol_renaming` and Chrome's `--use-webgpu-adapter=swiftshader` to see if a CPU adapter is deterministic. **Draft upstream note** (Dawn/Chromium, not three): 'Identical WebGPU command buffers (same pipeline, bind groups, buffers, no writes between submits) produce different fragment outputs for ~0.1 % of pixels between submits on Apple GPU / Metal; minimal case: instanced sphere grid with a PBR point-light shader; frame 0 after buffer creation differs from later frames, later frames flip when other GPU work is interleaved.' W7's residual tolerance in the time-slice checks stays as is (it still matches what this shows); the 'core lookup matches the pre-phase-2 TSL node (grid + mirror sphere)' check keeps max <= 1 and can still flake under load. |

| W12 | Fix: dark hole in examples/ssr mirrorWall's floor reflection behind the sphere; kit sky; speckles; composite environment term; timestamps leak | fix | `examples/shared/{kit,scenes,rooms}.ts` (volumes, sky, `low` view), `packages/three/src/sssr/composite.ts`, `packages/core/src/sssr/timer.ts`, `coverage` view in examples/ssr, the `low` check in run-headless `hybrid` | 5392 | done on branch `worktree-agent-a62351792f8dd58d9` (not merged) | **Root cause:** not the composite's inputs. At the hole px the composite already equalled three's probe term (sRGB 0.364 vs 0.361), and no target held NaN/Inf. Both were dim: the kit's probe boxes had the floor on their bottom face (mirrorWall 0.1 m inside, blend 0.5, so coverage 0.2), so the fallback was 80% environment, and `environmentIntensity = 0.3` scales three's whole IBL term, probes included. With a black sky, every miss read as a hole. **Fixes:** `floorProbe()` takes influence 0.6 m below the floor and keeps projection and capture via `projectionOffset` / `originOffset`; the wall-mounted mirrors in offscreen / facingMirrors / boxRoom get the same margin (D1500). Environment px in the source view: facingMirrors 31k to 0, offscreen 29k to 0, rooms 67. A procedural daylight sky (`useSky`, D1502) is background and environment at intensity 1. The composite uses the probes' own lookup with the atlas environment slot (D1501). PassTimer reuses unread readback buffers, with a test that fails before the fix. **Result (mirrorWall `low`):** hole mean 86 to 149/255 (probes-only 152, wall 187); check `hybrid mirrorWall low` passes. Shots `out/w12-low-{before,after}-{final,source,confidence}.png`, `out/w12-default-after-final.png`. Speckles: SSR noise on the r = 0.35 sphere, clean once denoised (backlog). `pnpm check` 144 PASS. The kit scenes changed, so ssr-gate.md's numbers predate this. |
| W15 | Phase 6, first part: babylon.js adapter (WebGPUEngine) proving the engine-agnostic core | code | `packages/babylon/`, `src/babylon.ts`, `examples/babylon/`, the babylon section of `scripts/run-headless.mjs` | 5395 | first pass done on branch `worktree-agent-a675925ef12cfc11f` (not merged) | **Built:** `@pmndrs/reflections/babylon` (private `@pmndrs/reflections-babylon`, bundled; `@babylonjs/core` 9.35.0 optional peer `>=9.35.0 <10` + dev dep). Device / textures: `engine._device`, `_hardwareTexture.underlyingResource` (the upscaler's two private fields), core outputs handed back with `wrapWebGPUTexture` (no copies; r32float wraps typed FLOAT so Babylon binds them unfilterable). `BabylonFrame` builds core `FrameInputs` from a `FrameGraphGeometryRendererTask` (rgba16f colour, depth32f, PREPASS_WORLD_NORMAL, PREPASS_REFLECTIVITY, PREPASS_VELOCITY_LINEAR): normal unpack 2 / -1, motionVectorScale [-1, -1] (GPU-checked: reprojection error 0.014 vs 0.21 unprojected), depthInverted from `useReverseDepthBuffer`; handedness and Y-flipped targets go through the matrices handed to the tracer (D1800, D1801); gloss -> roughness and F0 -> albedo + metalness 1 in one compute pass (D1802, D1803). `ScreenSpaceReflections` = core tracer on Babylon's device, fallback = core `probeRadianceFallback` over a `BabylonProbeSet` (core `ProbeSet`; Babylon `ReflectionProbe` captures through `BabylonCubeCapture`, which undoes Babylon's Y-mirrored WebGPU cubes, D1805; environment slot captured from the skybox) or a constant; `enabled = false` = classify with threshold 0 (probes / environment only). `StripSpecularIBLPlugin` (PBR material plugin) removes Babylon's specular IBL from the lit colour (`sceneColorSpecular: 'direct'`). `FrameGraphReflectionsTask` (dispatch) + `FrameGraphReflectionsCompositeTask` (WGSL post process: lit + Babylon BRDF-LUT split-sum weight x core radiance, 11 debug views; D1804, D1806). `examples/babylon`: mirrorWall (x negated into the left-handed world, so the framing matches `examples/ssr`), sky, sun shadows, SSR / probes toggles, debug views. `pnpm check -- --only babylon`: both variants 197895 rays, mirror floor 65.1% confident, orientation 24 / 24, motion [-1, -1] best, tracer 0.27 ms at 960x540, no validation errors. Build: the dts step now runs rollup with an 8 GB heap (OOM at the default with babylon's types). **Core contract needs (input for the lead), each logged:** (1) a handedness / view-forward convention (D1800); (2) a bottom-left texture-origin convention (D1801); (3) `roughnessUnpack {mul, add}` like normals, to drop the gloss pass (D1802); (4) an F0 input for specular-workflow G-buffers (D1803); (5) a capture transform on `ProbeSet` beyond `mirrorX` (D1805); (6) an option to take misses' fallback at the pixel's roughness, or the fallback as its own output, so a composite needs no probe maths (D1806); (7) a lookup variant engines can bind from their material systems: Babylon's WGSL processor knows `texture_cube_array` and `var<storage>`, but there is no public way to wrap core's atlas as a cube-array view or its table buffer as a Babylon `StorageBuffer`, so probes don't reach Babylon materials yet (a table-in-uniform or table-in-texture variant would also serve pixi). **Next:** probes in Babylon PBR (a material plugin injecting `probeLookupFunctionWgsl` once (7) is solved, or a per-probe cube export as `reflectionTexture`); `hitColor: 'history'` (needs last frame's composite as a texture); the denoise seam (`FfxReflectionDenoiser` on Babylon's device); RT when `@pmndrs/bvh` can take Babylon meshes; packed-subpath check for `./babylon` (SPEC phase 6 gate). **Pixi (next stream):** same shape: `packages/pixi` gets the device from pixi v8's WebGPU renderer (`renderer.gpu.device`) and GPU textures from its texture sources; pixi is 2-D, so the frame contract comes from a pixi-3d / custom mesh pass (no G-buffer of its own): either the pixi3d beta the upscaler uses or a minimal MRT pass the adapter owns; screen-space only (probes need a 3-D capture), composite as a pixi `Filter` in WGSL; check the Y orientation and handedness the same way (orientation + motion checks). |

Device / textures: `renderer.gpu.device`, `renderer.texture.getGpuSource()` (the upscaler adapter's two public calls); `requestReflectionsDevice()` for `app.init({ gpu })` adds `subgroups` + core's limits (pixi requests neither, D2003). **3-D layer decision:** not `@pixi/3d` (closed beta: must not be bundled or copied into public code, and it has no normal / roughness outputs; motion + depth only on the maintainer's branch), so the adapter owns a minimal one (D2000): `ReflectionScene` / `ReflectionMesh` / `ReflectionMaterial` (metal-rough; `map` / `emissiveMap` / `roughnessMap` are **pixi Textures**, so RenderTextures of pixi Text / Graphics / Sprites become lit, reflected surfaces, D2001) / `ReflectionCamera`, `Geometry` builders, one MRT pass (`GBufferRenderer`: sun GGX + PCF shadow map, hemisphere ambient, emissive, procedural sky) in the contract's **native** conventions: depth32float standard Z, raw normals with roughness in w, albedo + metalness, uv motion `motionVectorScale [1, 1]`, `sceneColorSpecular 'direct'` (none of D1800-D1803 needed). `PixiProbeSet` = core `ProbeSet`, captured by the same layer straight into WebGPU's cube orientation (no D1805 pass; environment slot = sky-only capture). `ScreenSpaceReflections` = core tracer on pixi's device + probe fallback, outputs repacked into pixi storage textures (D2002), optional denoiser through core's seam (`FfxReflectionDenoiser` in the example when the sibling Denoiser repo is there; D2006). Composite = `ReflectionsFilter`, a WGSL pixi `Filter` (Karis split-sum D2004, ACES, sRGB; 12 debug views; maps the G-buffer by filter-bounds position, D2005). `ReflectionsView` = a `Sprite` wiring it all (`view.render()` once per frame, before pixi renders the stage). **Demo** `examples/pixi`: neon arcade, three signs drawn by pixi each frame (Text with glow, Graphics equaliser, Graphics synthwave sun + stars), glossy tiled floor with a Graphics roughness map, skyline boxes with Graphics windows, mirror sphere, gold torus; SSR / probes / denoise / animate toggles, view select, a debug inset that is a second sprite with its own filter, pixi Text HUD, drag to orbit. `pnpm check -- --only pixi`: subgroups and workgroup variants 84293 rays, finite, glossy floor 47.8% / 48.4% confident, captures 24 / 24 directions, motion best at [1, 1] (0.064 vs 0.382 unprojected), tracer 0.27-0.79 ms at 960x540 (loop stopped, noisy), no validation errors; screenshots `out/pixi-*.png`. Build: dts heap 8 -> 12 GB (peak ~10 GB with pixi's types). **Core contract needs (input for the lead):** (1) an output allocator hook (D2002); (2) a shared split-sum / DFG term (D2004); (3) the seam taking an encoder (D2006); (4) misses' fallback at the pixel's roughness or as its own output (= W15's D1806, same gap: the pixi composite uses core's radiance as is); (5) W15's (7), a lookup engines can bind from their materials, is moot here (the adapter's own pass could bind core's set directly) but returns with `@pixi/3d`. Not needed for pixi: handedness / Y-origin flags (D1800 / D1801) and a capture transform (D1805), because the adapter draws in the contract's conventions. **Next:** a `fromPixi3D` frame builder once `@pixi/3d` publishes depth / normal / roughness / velocity outputs; `hitColor: 'history'` (feed last frame's composite); RT when `@pmndrs/bvh` takes the layer's geometry; packed-subpath check for `./pixi`. |
| W22 | Fix: the wall's pattern showing through the reflected sphere (examples/ssr mirrorWall, low near the floor): depth-aware hybrid blend | fix | **Files touched (for the merge):** `packages/three/src/sssr/composite.ts` (blend), `packages/three/src/sssr/HybridReflections.ts` (shared `depthAware` uniform, source view uses `ssrWeight`), `packages/three/src/sssr/ScreenSpaceReflectionsPass.ts` (+1 uniform `viewProjection`, set in `render`), `packages/three/src/probes/ReflectionProbesNode.ts` (+`ReflectionProbesProxyNode` / `sampleReflectionProbesProxyShare`), `packages/three/src/index.ts` (export), `packages/core/src/probes/lookup.ts` (+`pr_parallaxDistance`, `probeProxyShareFunctionWgsl`, module `probeProxyShare`), `packages/core/src/index.ts` (export), `examples/shared/scenes.ts` (`lowSphere` view), `examples/ssr/src/main.ts` (views `ssrWeight` / `hitFade` / `probeConsistency`, Depth-aware checkbox, `?depthAware=0`, `__p.setDepthAware`, `__p.blendStats()`), the `lowSphere` check in run-headless `hybrid`. No denoiser-seam or tracer changes. | 5401 | done on branch `worktree-agent-a4bce1040d8091a65` (not merged) | **Cause confirmed, with one correction to the brief.** At the new kit view `lowSphere` (camera at the sphere's centre height, 1.8 m away), floor px whose exact reflection hits the sphere (raycast) mostly have partial confidence. Their rays meet the sphere's underside, which the camera can't see, so the march stops up to the thickness behind its visible front. The composite gave the rest, `1 - c`, to the box-projected probe, which shows the wall's D3/D4/D5 cells, plus a ghost of the probe's own capture of the sphere. The denoiser only smooths this (raw and denoised are alike). The correction: the vignette is ~1 there, so it is the thickness falloff, not the edge fade. Mean probe weight on those 41464 px: 0.048. **Fix (D2400, D2401):** the composite splits FFX's confidence into the screen-edge fade (the vignette, recomputed at the hit) and the thickness part. The thickness part's remainder goes to the probe only for the share of the probe term whose box-projected surface is not behind the hit (core `probeProxyShare` over the volume table). The edge fade still blends to the probe (no pop, D41), rejected rays still take it, and the room's own walls (probe surface = hit) keep FFX's blend. The signal the denoiser takes uses the new weight, so raw and denoised agree. **Numbers (lowSphere, 960x540):** sphere px probe weight 0.048 -> 0.004 (denoise off: same). Cost: px reflecting the wall *behind* the sphere (the probe was right) take hit weight 0.009 -> 0.095 (low 0.058 -> 0.15, default 0.06 -> 0.11): a slightly crisper, spikier top edge on the reflection. Other kit scenes, A/B in one page: < 1.2% of px change by > 8/255 (explainer, glossy floor). With `?rt=1` every uncertain ray is traced, so the rule changes nothing (sphere probe weight 0). **No regressions:** W12 `low` hole mean 149.3/255 (W12: 149); W9 stripes cube 1.9 / sphere 8.1 (92.5% confident; core outputs, untouched); `pnpm check --only ssr`, `hybrid`, `ssr-rt` all PASS; typecheck, test, build OK. Shots: `out/w22-{before,after}-{final,confidence,source,raw}.png` ('before' = `depthAware` 0 in the same build, which is exactly the old blend; 'after-confidence' is the `ssrWeight` view), `out/hybrid-mirrorWall-lowSphere-final.png`. **Left (D2402):** rays rejected by thickness (c = 0) that truly hit a hidden face still take the probe: a thin crescent near the contact. Making them hits would streak 88% of the behind px. Also left: the probe ghost of the sphere. RT fixes both; a back-face depth pass would fix the first without rays. |

Deleted geometry.ts, scene.ts, math.ts, gbuffer.ts (975 lines). **The beta never enters the repo or the package:** the adapter talks to pixi-3d through structural types and plain shader data (`pixi3d.ts`); the one beta-dependent class (`ReflectionsGBufferPass`, a `Pass3D`) plus `reflectiveMaterial()` and `ProbeCapture` live in `examples/pixi3d/shared/reflections.ts`; `@pixi/3d` resolves from the gitignored `examples/pixi3d/vendor/` (vite alias + a `pixi3d-optional` plugin serving `unavailable.ts`, the setup notice, without it); root `tsconfig` excludes `examples/pixi3d`, which has its own (`pnpm exec tsc -p examples/pixi3d`). `dist/pixi.js` has no `@pixi/3d` import. `meshoptimizer` added as a devDependency (the beta's one runtime dep). **pixi-3d provides:** scene colour (View3D into an rgba16float RenderTexture, `toneMapping: 'custom'`), `DepthOutputPass`, `MotionVectorPass`, `Camera3D` matrices, `MeshPass` + `Camera3D.renderTexture` (probe faces), `ContainerTexture` (pixi content on 3-D planes), `extraBits`. **Contract items it can't provide (closest input used, D2101 / D2102):** (1) normals, roughness, metalness, albedo: no G-buffer output and pass shaders can't read the material struct or textures; `REFLECTIONS_GBUFFER_SHADER` redraws the opted-in meshes reading material store texels 0 / 1 directly (constants only: no base-colour / roughness / normal maps, no flat shading, vertex normals; relies on beta.2's internal store layout); (2) lit colour without the environment's specular: `REFLECTIONS_SCENE_COLOR_BIT` (Material3D `extraBits`) subtracts iblBit's single-scatter term (needs an environment; materials without it double-count; GPU-checked: white metal under a white sky 0.0019 with it, 1.0 without); (3) a cube capture with background: local probes see black sky (environment slot is right); (4) depth32float output (we copy depth24plus-stencil8 to r32float); (5) BRDF LUT access for a Filter (Karis fit stays, D2004). Note for the pixi team: `docs/research/pixi3d-needs.md`. **Demo** `examples/pixi3d/arcade`: W18's neon arcade rebuilt in pixi-3d (signs are pixi Text / Graphics through `ContainerTexture`, glossy floor r 0.06, mirror sphere, gold torus, skyline with Graphics windows, sun with shadows, hemisphere sky), SSR / probes / denoise / animate toggles, view select, debug inset, HUD. **Checks** `pnpm check -- --only pixi3d` (vendor present): subgroups + workgroup 80496 rays, finite, glossy floor 45.7% / 46.4% confident; G-buffer values (8920 mirror px, 4156 gold px); contract camera reprojects onto pixi-3d's pixels at 0.0011 px, G-buffer normals vs depth normals 0.9976; scene-colour bit 0.0019 vs 1.0; probe captures 24 / 24 directions; motion best at [1, 1] (0.064 vs 0.382 without); tracer 0.18-0.34 ms total at 960x540 (loop stopped, noisy; one run read 2.3 ms total with the passes summing to 0.28); no validation errors. Without vendor: the notice renders (checked). API stays #35046-compatible. **Numbers:** cube-array vs the hw-cube reference mean 0.0000, max 0 / 255 (warp vs hw: mean 0.051, max 3); probes feature checks green on both layouts (default cube array; `--probes-layout cube2d`). Lookup at 1080p, measured once (loop stopped, frame minus no-IBL, 432-sphere grid): wall warp 0.113 ms vs cube-array 0.090 ms (1.26x), GPU timestamps 0.192 vs 0.133 ms (1.44x); legacy-warp 0.110 / 0.189. Readout: PT to N spp, glossy mask from our G-buffer, MAD / bias / temporal std / orbit flicker (2nd temporal difference, 0.05 deg/frame) in 8-bit display units; `__p.flickerMap(id)`. `pnpm check -- --only showcase-compare` passes. **Tool bugs found:** three's `CubeMapNode` caches equirect->cube per texture, not per renderer: a second renderer sharing the showcase's cached HDR bakes black probes (looked exactly like 'RT changes everything'); each method loads its own HDRI. three-gpu-pathtracer renders clearcoat black on ToyCar / CarConcept (local and upstream); reference drops clearcoat (D2303). Both of our modes blur the HDRI in near-mirror floors (misses take the 128 px probe; probes-only bias is the worst), which is why three + FFX has the lower MAD on the studio stages (5.2 / 15.7 / 3.7 vs 16.7 / 21.0 / 14.1); in Sponza three's shaded floor reflects the sky (64.7 vs 26.9). **Boiling: ours does not boil more than three SSR + FFX here.** Still std (8-bit luma) ours 0.03-0.14 vs 0.15-0.65 on all four scenes and a 0.25-roughness floor; orbit flicker tied (toyCar 0.17/0.20, car 0.33/0.33, Sponza 2.28/2.22, glossy floor 0.33/0.34 and 1.36/1.36 at 0.5 deg/f), chess 0.25 vs 0.16 (on pieces + board top; unchanged by motion x0-2, spq 4, hit=current, tsf 0.95; three's lit image has no env specular so its floor is lower). Denoiser inputs match the demo's conventions (perceptual roughness, world ray length with 0 -> far, world normals, NDC motion, tsf 0.7): no bug, nothing fixed. The demo is calm by content and post: roughness forced 0.3, no normal maps, mirrorBias 0.5 (0 raises three+FFX std 0.149 -> 0.230), TRAA + sharpen + grade. Structural gap: 1 ray per quad without FFX's variance-guided extra rays (seam passes no variance; `FfxReflectionDenoiser` keeps it private): spq 4 cuts denoised std 0.14 -> 0.10 and fast-orbit flicker 1.36 -> 1.29 on the glossy floor. ## Platform

- **three floor: r187** (raised from r186 on 2026-11-10; `three@^0.187.0`, peer `>=0.187.0`). r187 still can't bind `texture_cube_array` in TSL. Its PMREMGenerator writes cube mips (#34585). In r187 a render target's cube texture lists every level in `mipmaps`, base included; r186 listed only the levels past 0.

## Fine-tune backlog

Ranked view: [`TRIAGE.md`](TRIAGE.md). This section keeps the detail behind each row.

For the fine-tune pass once the phases are built and running. Nothing here blocks a phase or a decision; if one starts to, promote it. One line each, with a pointer.

**Tracked fixes (known fix, waiting its turn; pick these up before new work in the same area)**
- Transmissive glass casts shadow-map shadows in the examples (W54, D3682): `examples/shared/gltf.ts` sets `castShadow` on every mesh, so carConcept's cabin is shadowed where the PT lets the key light through (+4.6 on the glass px when off). Skip `castShadow` for materials with transmission > 0.
- ~~Settings guide (maintainer, 2026-11-12, on `hitLobeFilter`: "document/note this and provide it as an option", default stays 1): a user-facing `docs/SETTINGS.md` listing each tuning knob with its default, what it trades and when to change it (`hitLobeFilter` 0 / 1 / 1.5 with W45's numbers, `hitShading`, `mirrorBias`, the RT roughness threshold, `probeSpecularOcclusion`, the examples' `?specAA`); plus a showcase panel slider and `?lobeFilter=` on the showcase (only compare.html has it today). Also from the maintainer's answers (decisions page, 2026-11-12): (a) give every option its GPU cost, since small costs add up and a later 'fast' preset will need them (`?specAA` is about 0.1-0.15 ms at 1280x720); (b) make the visibility ray on RT-re-shaded on-screen hits (D3542, off by default) a setting and document it, for apps with mirror-in-mirror scenes like `facingMirrors`.~~ Done (W51): `docs/SETTINGS.md`, generated from the tuning bench's schema (`pnpm settings:doc`, checked by `test/settings-doc.test.ts`), every knob with its default, trade-off and GPU cost; `?lobeFilter=` and a "Hit lobe filter" slider on the showcase; every knob on `examples/showcase/bench.html`. (b) the D3542 visibility ray is W52's `traceSettings.hitShadeVisibility: 'all'`, on the bench and in SETTINGS.md with W52's other halo options.
- ~~**W47 (reflections-09) owns this.** RT hit shading over a mixed-size probe set (W60, D4702): the three trace path binds only `probes.texture` and packs `packReflectionProbesTable`'s 'primary' form, so probes of another size take zero weight at traced hits / misses. Fix in the trace lane: bind `probes.textures` (core `probeHitShading` resources take the extra views, lookup `atlasBinding: 4` past its uniform), compile with `atlases` / `volumeAtlases: probes.bakedAtlases`, pack with `{ atlases: 'all' }`, key the shading on the atlas signature.~~ Done (W47, D4702): both trace providers, plus core `probeHitShading` over a mixed-size ProbeSet (did not compile).
- ~~Probe lookup cost grows with the volume count, not the sizes (W61): three unrolls every volume into every lit material and the composite, each a box test and two table reads per px whether it covers the px or not; 33 volumes add ~10-13 ms at 1080p (rooms 10.2, offscreen 9.4, Sponza 12.7 over the probe-free frame). Fix (packages, probes lane): skip a volume's reads once `remaining` is 0 and cull volumes per draw (an object's bounds against the influence boxes, so a material compiles only the volumes it can touch), or a per-tile / clustered volume list as HDRP does. Measure with `--only probe-layouts-measure` (W61_PARTS=timing).~~ **Done by W63 (D4950):** a world-space volume grid in the table, and the lookup walks only P's cell above 4 volumes (32 volumes 9.6 -> ~0.8 ms on rooms, bit-identical probe term); `docs/research/probe-lookup-cost.md`.
- ~~Sampled textures per stage with mixed probe sizes (D4804; W61 + W47, one item for both cases). Each probe size is one more atlas binding wherever probes are looked up. (1) three materials (W61): Sponza's glTF materials with a three-size set need 18 > WebGPU's default 16 and are not drawn. (2) core SSSR (W47): with a 4-size ProbeSet as both radiance fallback and `hitSpecularShading`, the intersect binds 18 (counted, not run); the trace pass reaches 14. Fix: request the adapter's `maxSampledTexturesPerShaderStage` in `reflectionsRequiredLimits` (the examples' layout pages already do it themselves); add a sampled-texture count to core's stage-limit check (`checkStageLimits`, via a `sampledTextureCount` on HitShading / RadianceFallback) and run it on the SSSR intersect too, which checks nothing today; and have the bake warn when atlases + a material's own textures pass the device limit.~~ Done (W62, D4900): the 18 was three's composite binding every atlas twice (two lookups, two texture nodes); the lookups now share one node per atlas, so Sponza's three-size set fits the default 16 (composite 15). `reflectionsRequiredLimits` asks for up to 32; core counts sampled textures in the trace pass and the SSSR intersect (`sampledTextureCount` on the seams); three's bake warns once at >= 3 sizes on a 16-limit device.
- ~~SSSR with a ProbeSet as both radiance fallback and `hitSpecularShading` fails WGSL parsing (D4901, W62): the probe lookup module is included at group 1 and again at group 3 ('redeclaration of pl_faceDirUV'), at any atlas count, so the combination has never run (three's pass uses a constant fallback; babylon / pixi no hit shading). Fix: emit the lookup's shared chunks once per module (`dedupeChunks`, as the layouts do) and prefix the set-level functions per group, in packages/core/src/probes/lookup.ts (W63's file; W63 is merged, so this is unblocked); `--only regressions` 'sssr limits' accepts the error by name until then.~~ **Done by W64 (D4901):** the includes are marked chunks the SSSR / trace passes keep once (`dedupeWgslChunks`), the hit shading's names carry `_g3`; the combined frame = the fallback seam alone (0 px, 1 and 4 atlases), 'sssr limits' now a real pass.
- Probe table upload (W63): three re-packs and re-uploads the whole table every render, now with the grid (+64 KB per set, ~13-41 us CPU); skip both when the volumes did not change (a version on ReflectionProbes), and the RT hit shading's own copy too.
- Two probe walks per lit material (W63): three's radiance and irradiance contexts each walk the volumes at the same P (a third with clear coat); one walk returning both, or the weights shared, halves the materials' share. The composite's lookup, its coverage lookup and the proxy share are three more walks at one P.
- Re-time `PROBE_UNROLL_MAX_VOLUMES` (4) on a quiet GPU and on a discrete GPU (W63: unrolled vs grid cross between 4 and 8 volumes on Apple M-series; most W63 runs shared the GPU).
- Check flake (W63): `--only probes` 'environment reaches the image, and removing it clears the slot' failed once with removed max 88 / 255 on a loaded GPU, passed with max 0 on the rerun (like the time-slice residual over point-light highlights).
- Sphere fringe in reflections (D4303), RT off only: W44 measured that RT already removes it (red px outside the RT silhouette: SSR-only 50, SSR + re-shade 35, RT 0, RT + re-shade 2), so the earlier 'thin thickness when RT is on' idea is dropped. Without RT, FFX's thin 0.015 cuts a hole in the sphere; the fix there is a back-face depth layer (one more depth target and a thickness test against it). Maintainer agreed to track it (2026-11-11, Q4).
- A path-traced ground truth for the moving and mirror scenes (maintainer 2026-11-11, Q2): `?rt=1` isn't ground truth where traced hits are coloured from the probes (D901) or one mirror blocks another (D3513). Reuse the compare page's WebGPU path tracer (W21/W23) on the ssr kits, then retune W37/W38 thresholds against it.
- W52 (D3642): tune `hitShadeOcclusion`'s march (length 0.3 d, thickness 0.15 d, 8 steps; carConcept hero Measure +0.19, likely false occlusion behind thin parts) before it is considered as a default.
- W52 (D3641): `hitLobeFilterMixed` reads last frame's hit source without reprojection (one frame late in motion); and the settings guide (tracked fix above) should list W52's three settings with their trade-offs.
- W53 (D3663): with the denoiser's hit source on, source edges show the tracer's per-quad copy (2x2 stair step on carConcept's lip at 960x540); try an edge-aware expansion of the per-quad source, or more rays per quad along mixed edges.
- Texture-limit support check, material side (maintainer 2026-11-12): core passes throw at build with a clear message (W62 `checkStageLimits`), but a three material whose own maps plus the probe atlases pass `maxSampledTexturesPerShaderStage` still fails inside three's pipeline creation with no message of ours. Warn at bake / `reflectionProbes()` time: count each lit material's sampled textures plus the set's atlases against `renderer.backend.device.limits`.
- W53 (bench): on the Denoiser main checkout (no PR pmndrs/denoiser#29) the bench's `hitSource` / `hitDistW` controls are silent no-ops (`setOptions` missing). Grey them out with a "needs denoiser#29" tag when the denoiser lacks `setOptions`, until #29 merges.
- W53 (D3661 / D3662): the affinity resolve costs +0.35-0.5 ms at 1080p (hit-distance weight +1.3): one 81-tap loop now accumulates two moment sets; cheaper with a 5x5 affine estimate or an LDS-cached exp. The denoiser repo owns the shader (PR pmndrs/denoiser#29).
- pixi composite drops material occlusion on reflections (D4512); fix: multiply the added-back term by the material occlusion the scene-colour bit removed it with: no test scene has occlusion maps yet.
- **W45 owns this.** ~~Specular occlusion near mirrors (D4513, W39)~~: done with RT by W45 (D3551: traced misses skip Lagarde; contact low pose r < 0.1 12.96 -> 3.96 vs the traced reference). RT off still darkens near-mirror misses beside lit objects (W39: 23.8 -> 33.4); a confidence- or hit-distance-weighted SO for untraced misses is the open half.

- Blended seams between neighbouring probes (W38, D4401): floor-quadrant probes lower the showcase error (carConcept low 15.9 -> 9.1) but show hard seams and smeared copies of the hero. With blended seams, a fitted floor probe could show the hero in the RT-off floor. Maintainer parked it beside per-probe resolution (2026-11-12).

- W51 (D3624): @pmndrs/upscaler keeps presenting its first frame when its GPU timer's query set fails to allocate (Metal "Cannot allocate sample buffer" with several renderers' timers on a page); the compare / bench methods run it without a timer. Report upstream, then turn the bench's upscaler timings back on.
- W51 (D3620): three's SSR + ffxReflections at an upscale ratio (scene pass at the render size) throws a radiance / guide size mismatch; the bench keeps three's side at ratio 1.

**Correctness polish (not visible yet)**
- contact under the upscaler drifts at rest (W39): frame 120 vs 240 with the camera still, mean 1.5/255, 3% px > 8/255 in blocks on the floor reflection of the cylinder and torus (AA off: 0.12, 0.04%). Jitter changes which rays validate; the 128-frame history doesn't settle it. Time-boxed, not chased.

- About 1,800 extra forward-Z misses on the sssr-core glossy floor (W9 row).
- Reflected sphere stripe ratio still 8.0 (cube 1.7) after D1000; streaks behind objects 4.6% with the hybrid thickness (D1001). Visible in examples/ssr mirrorWall as a jagged comb on the reflected sphere's top edge, raw and denoised (W12).
- Raw glossy SSR (denoise off) speckles the r = 0.35 sphere, rim included, with single-pixel hits (the red post, the white wall); no NaN/Inf in any target at 6 poses, and denoised it's clean at `default` and `low` (W12).
- ~~RT hit shading reads 0.67-0.91x the on-screen colour~~: fixed by W28 (D901: hits take the probes three lights the scene with; 0.96-1.08x now). What is left is per-pixel material (D902): mean abs rel 0.32-0.40 from the patterned walls; toyCar 4.5x too bright from its averaged emissive map.
- The three adapter's probe hit shading packs its own copy of the probe volume table (D2900): share the probe node's once `packages/three/src/probes/` exports it.

- `pnpm check -- --only probes --probes-layout cube2d` logs a fragment compile error ('probesLookup' expects `texture_cube_array`, gets `texture_2d_array`) after the shim is uninstalled at run time: three materials compiled for the cube-array bake. Pre-existing at `0ac23d4` (W35 bisected); the default run is clean.
- Compatibility mode (D4100): the probe bake still fails there because the downsampler reads and writes mips of one texture in one dispatch. Everything else on the compat path is in (W35).
- Babylon and pixi-3d could hand their raw captures to core's capture transform (`CAPTURE_BABYLON_FACES` / `CAPTURE_CAMERA_FACES`, D1805 / D2104) and drop their own fix-up passes; W35 left the adapters as they are.

**Quality experiments**
- W54 (D3681): the specular lobe of hits inside a closed wheel is open (one visibility ray along R at most). carConcept's chrome hub reads 44 vs the PT's 28, and the floor-reflected wheel 47.5 vs 32. A cone / multi-ray visibility for wide lobes, or a probe per wheel well; the denoiser adds +23 on the chrome px. **W55:** a probe per wheel well closes the hub rect (44.0 -> 27.0, PT 28.1) and the floor-reflected wheel (32.7 vs 32.1); the chrome star itself only half (100 -> 74.8, PT 50.5): lobe visibility is still the fix for that half.
- W58 (D3740): the reflected silhouette inside one surface (the band's lower edge on carConcept's floor) still steps 2x2 at spq 1 with `quadCopy: 'sameSurface'`; edge-aware upsampling of the per-quad result. Also tune the same-surface test (5 % depth, normal dot 0.9) and measure 'sameSurface' on chess / toyCar / Sponza before a default.
- W58 (D3741): three's clear-coat env reflection is never subtracted; a thin bright line on grazing coated bevels (carConcept's skirt edge). A coat IBL term in the composite if it matters.
- W56 (D3720): three's geometry roughness on small chrome bevels (carConcept's star 100.1 -> 78.6 without it, PT 50.5; paint flicker +1-4%). Decide `geometryRoughness` off / 0.25 for ours, or replace three's term with a variance-form specular AA (Tokuyoshi 2019) next to specAA.
- W56 (D3721, denoiser repo): FFX's spatial filter spreads the spokes into carConcept's chrome star (faces 24.1 raw -> 56.3 denoised, PT 29.3). Harder roughness / normal weights for near-mirror centres, or no spatial pass below an alpha.
- W54 (D3680): wheel interiors and the cabin are three's unoccluded IBL (disc 44.8 base / 49.4 ours / PT 18; cabin without glass 64 / 68.5 / PT 30). Try local probes in the wells and the cabin; the cabin probe needs per-material exclusion so the glass's outer reflection doesn't read it. **W55:** wheel probes take the disc / caliper 53.1 -> 26.6 (PT 18.0); a cabin probe inside the glass barely moves the cabin (glass hidden 68.5 -> 64.6, PT 29.8) - next is what lights the seats in three vs the PT, or AO on three's diffuse.
- Shadow-ray traversal order (D2601): @pmndrs/bvh main f535415 compiles `anyHitOrder: 'far' | 'larger' | 'near'` into the walk (`traceModuleWgsl`). bvh measured 'larger' at 1.39-1.73x three-mesh-bvh on any-hit rays from outside geometry, but 0.78x on merged-tree surface rays. Time it on our hit shadow rays (`traceAny`, W25 / D903; 'moving' and Sponza at 1080p) before switching; the hits never change, only the cost. The same rebuild makes the CPU refit 2.8-3.6x faster (matters once we wire `setPoseCpu`, D1703).
- Sponza vs the path tracer (maintainer, 2026-11-12): this package is not a path tracer or GI; Sponza's shade bias is raster lighting (no GI), not reflections. Judge reflection changes there on reflection-dominated pixels or as deltas against reflections off, so the lighting gap doesn't hide or fake them; W46's Sponza + RT +1.3 is accepted on that basis.
- Without RT, the re-shade (W44) has no occlusion: reflected wheels, rims and the underside read lighter than the PT. Maintainer: backlog (2026-11-12).
- Examples' GTAO (D4510) has no temporal filter or blur: its grain reaches rough surfaces undenoised when `?specOcc=1`; three's denoise node or the TRAA-friendly temporal mode would smooth it. 1080p cost not measured cleanly.
- RT hit shading vs probe size: with 256 px probes (the default since W31) contact's traced hits read 1.10x the on-screen brightness with @pmndrs/bvh (1.084 at 128 px; mirrorWall / moving unchanged within 0.01). Both sides brighten; the hits' roughness-1 lookup moves more. The ssr-rt checks pin `probeRes=128` (run-headless `url`) until this is understood.
- The SSR-to-probe step on glossy floors is mostly radiance, not blur: hit vs probe luma bias -10 to -14/255 on mirrorWall, +6 to +23 on contact / the strips (`pnpm check --only seam`, W26). Lagarde's options close 4-24 % of it. Look at hit shading vs the probe capture's lighting (D903's dark bias may be the same thing).
- ~~Probe count vs resolution (D23)~~: measured by W61 (SPEC 'Choosing probe sizes', `docs/research/w61/`): at equal memory a few large probes fitted to the rooms, or mixed sizes with 512 on mirror-grade floors, beat a 32 x 128 grid on rooms, offscreen and Sponza (vs the path tracer too), and 33 volumes cost ~10-13 ms at 1080p. Left open: the grid's capture points (cell centres next to columns paint dark blocks into the floor; a capture placed clear of geometry was not tried) and a cheaper lookup for many volumes (tracked fix).
- Probe captures lit by our environment slot vs three's PMREM (W31, D3201): on mirrorWall a re-bake with the probe node installed gives probes ~8 / 255 luma darker than the first bake, whose captures used three's PMREM of the sky as IBL; compare the two roughness-1 irradiances of the same sky.
- Probe fallback behind objects is the probe's blurred, parallax-shifted view (one capture point): a soft capsule above the sphere's reflection in examples/ssr mirrorWall `low` (W12, out/w12-low-after-final.png). `?rt=1` fills it.
- The kit's sky is procedural (`useSky`, D1502); W11's Poly Haven HDRIs would read better if the kit ever takes downloads.
- ~~Mirror bias under motion (D49, W32)~~: measured by W39 under the upscaler in a pendulum orbit: no gain with the denoiser, default stays 0 (`docs/research/w39/mirror-bias-upscaler.json`).
- ~~FFX's roughness gate is fixed at construction; the examples/ssr threshold slider doesn't move it (D1402)~~: fixed by W34 (the page rebuilds the denoiser on change). Left: a slider drag rebuilds it (pipelines + the hybrid signal material) once per frame while the value moves; a reconfigure call in the denoiser repo would make it free.
- Lookups below r = 0.2: the opt-in `'alpha'` lod mapping cuts p99 from 44.5 to 19.7/255; try an extra mip (D101, D806).
- BC6H split: compress the sharp mips, keep the rough tail uncompressed. Passes the gate on paper (1.71×, 130.5 KiB/probe); needs a two-texture lookup (ssr-gate.md / probe-layouts.md).
- Re-fit basis SH9 vs L1 (D60). `octa-hemi` layout. A real capture in the probe bench (W1).
- Probe resolution default 128 vs Lagarde's 256 (D23, D62). Unreal's roughness-to-mip mapping unverified (D3).

**Showcase (W11): what looks wrong to the eye** (`examples/showcase`; W17 checked W11's first five)
- Car concept: 'off' keeps the clearcoat rims (clearcoat IBL not modelled, D603-D606). (W17: the floor gap was the loose bounding box, fixed.)
- Glossy SSR without the denoiser speckles car paint black: validated hits on the dark sill / tyres, most only inside the scene's 0.046 + 2.3 r thickness (W17 row: 3.3% -> 1.1% of paint px at 0.015). A thinner test swaps them for the probe, which is bright where the truth (under the car) is dark; the RT fallback would answer them. The probe for the stage scenes is a compromise: one far-projected box, no contact parallax.
- Thickness is in view-space units (metres at the AMD sample's Sponza); scenes far from room scale set it per scene. A depth- or scene-relative default is untried. DiligentFX tests `distance / viewZ` against 0.025; a `thickness += k·|viewZ|` term with default 0 keeps FFX bit for bit (planned as X1 #2 in `docs/plans/ssr-sources-experiments.md`).
- Sponza: only the ground-floor `Floors` material is polished; the galleries stay rough. Alpha-tested foliage is opaque in the BVH (not seen yet, likely).
- ~~Outside every probe the 'off' baseline isn't black on mirrors~~: fixed by W12 (D1501, the composite uses the atlas's environment slot). The showcase's floor-covering boxes can stay.
- ~~examples/ssr rooms floor reads 'environment'~~: fixed by W12 (D1500, kit volumes reach 0.6 m below the floor; rooms 67 environment px left).
- Compare tool (W23): the W21 readout tables (`docs/research/showcase-compare.md`) were measured against the WebGL tracer with clearcoat dropped; re-run them against the WebGPU + OIDN reference (clearcoat in) when the numbers next matter. A 256 spp denoised reference is also OIDN-biased (D2305): for a decision on a sub-8-bit difference raise Reference samples or toggle Show denoised off.

- Near-mirror floors show the HDRI blurred: misses (SSR and RT) take the 128 px probe, the path tracer and three's SSR show it sharp (W21, `docs/research/showcase-compare.md` 1). Not D1806 (W30): three's composite already looks misses up at the pixel's roughness; the floor is too *blurry* because the probe atlas is 128 px a face, where D1806 was Babylon / pixi being too *sharp*. Fix in three's probe / environment path (a full-resolution environment past the probes, or a larger environment slot), not in core's tracer.
- SSR-only leaves a white probe-filled gap between the car and its floor reflection (rays under the car); the path tracer and RT show the dark underside (W21). W22's blend work.
- Variance-guided tracing is off (no variance from the denoiser through the seam): one ray per quad everywhere (W21: spq 4 lowers denoised std 0.14 -> 0.10 on a 0.25 floor).
- TAA (W40) softens the image: a sharpen pass like the denoiser demo's, or a lower jitter spread. Its motion is per pixel (no closest-depth dilation): thin edges over a moving background can ghost (D3500).
- W36: the upscaler runs RCAS off at ratio 1 (D4201); with `?upscale=` a sharpness > 0 may read better (not measured). The reactive mask idea (D4202) wants a reflection-aware motion instead; and a transmission flag in the G-buffer to mark glass.
- W37: the showcase stages (cars, chess on a mirror floor) capture their hero objects into the probe box; the same smear question as D4301, not measured. The `?rt=1` miss-fill metric depends on the probes through hit shading (D901): a path-traced reference for moving / facingMirrors. The reflected-sphere fringe (D4303): a thinner test when RT is on. A time-sliced bake with bounces 2 only re-bounces the slice (D4300).
- ~~W38: probe resolution is per set~~: per-probe since W60 (D4700); offscreen's floor and wall-mirror probes are 512 (D4701).
- W60: raw SSR (denoise off) shows single-pixel speckles on offscreen's floor when the floor probe is 512 px (all-512 too, so not the atlases; the 'noSSR' probe term is clean): the SSR / probe blend of near-mirror hits against a sharper probe. Not chased.
- W60: offscreen's floor at 1024 px reads worse than at 512 (17.59 vs 17.53, the ring's warm px 4074 vs 3646); unexplained, not chased.
- W60: ProbeSet's extra atlases sit at bindings 3.. of its group; a module that puts something else at 3 next to a multi-size set (core `probeHitShading`'s uniform) must pass `atlasBinding` (D4702). (W47: `probeHitShading` does, at 4.)
- W47: one `pnpm check` over every section passes the runner's 10 min limit (`process.exit(3)` 'timed out' after ssr-rt-bvh); sections run one by one all pass. Raise the limit or split the default run.
- W38: the stage probe on the showcase floors never shows the hero to the floor (capture above the model, far box). If the maintainer wants the hero in the RT-off floor, the low-quadrant idea (D4401) needs blended seams (a blend distance across the quadrant faces) and capture points nearer the floor they serve; untried beyond the one layout.
- W38: examples/probes 'bake slices between renders of one frame: the view keeps its own shadows' flaked once (slice 1: max 17 / 255 over 5 px, 0.001 %), passed on the rerun; W38 touched nothing in examples/probes or packages.
- W38: timings this session were taken with other sessions on the GPU (p90 up to 10x the median); the offscreen re-bake and render-pass numbers in D4400 are medians of two orders. Re-time on a quiet machine when the probe-count guidance matters (HANDOFF 'Probe count vs resolution').
- W38: `?rt=1` hit shading reads the probes (D901) and its traced misses take the probe under test, so W37's miss-fill metric moves with the probes it judges; D4402's fixed reference (misses -> environment, hits lit by a fixed set) is better but still black on mirror-in-mirror (D3513). A path-traced reference is the real fix (W37 backlog).
- The rest shimmer left without TAA is the GGX lobe noise on near-mirrors (W40): D49's bias knob (0.7: 0.31 -> 0.14/255 on the carConcept floor) and a longer denoiser history for near-mirrors. Counterpoint: W32 (the divergence lead's stream) measured D49 with the denoiser on a still camera and recommends keeping the default 0 (4–11 levels of bias for little noise gain); moving camera not measured. Compare both before changing the default.
- Traced hits' specular lobe has one visibility ray (D3513) and no other occlusion: the reflected tyres and underside in carConcept's floor read lighter than the PT with RT (hero +0.11 over main). A cone of visibility rays, or the probes' own occlusion, would tighten it.
- Screen-space re-shade (W44, D3540) has no occlusion: hits whose reflected ray R is blocked (carConcept's reflected wheels / rims and underside) read lighter than the PT; a screen-space visibility march along R, or the probe's own occlusion, would tighten it. toyCar + RT Measure +0.22 with it (11.06 -> 11.28), not chased.
- The `clearcoat` G-buffer target (W44, D3541) is not timed (4 B / px, the 8th attachment); the coat is shaded with the base normal (flake normal map), not three's clear coat normal.
- carConcept floor reflection: a dark band below the reflected sill where the PT shows the red lower door. SSR 'hits' the sill's front face for rays that pass behind it (confidence ~1); a back-face depth layer or tracing near-silhouette hits with RT would separate them (W41, D2402).
- ~~With RT the lower door is darker than the probe and the PT (W41)~~: W45: the denoiser clipped the lobe's rare bright floor-reflected samples (fixed by `hitLobeFilter`, D3550: RT door 111 -> 155); the rest (155 vs the PT's 197) is three's flake normal-map filtering (D3552), not reflections.
- ~~carConcept paint vs the PT (W45, D3552)~~: W46 built roughness from normal variance in the examples (D3570, `?specAA`, default on): door / skirt 155 / 42 -> 194 / 53 vs the PT's 197 / 60.
- specAA (W46, D3570): no geometric (normal-derivative, Kaplanyan 2016) term, so curved glossy edges still alias; Sponza + RT is 1.3 MAD worse with it (polished floor blurs at distance) inside Sponza's own shade bias vs the PT, re-measure once that bias is gone; traced hits use the coarsest-mip value per mesh (D3571).
- The FFX reflection denoiser loses 26-46 % of a heavy-tailed glossy signal's energy (W45, D3550; carConcept skirt, 64-frame linear means raw vs denoised): report to the denoiser repo; `hitLobeFilter` only lowers the variance it gets. `hitLobeFilter` 1 double-blurs the lobe (x1.41 alpha); the exact split (VNDF at alpha sqrt(1 - f), prefilter the rest) is D3550's revisit.

**W70 (X0) findings, logged not chased (2026-11-13, `docs/research/w70/README.md`)**
- The line-spread gate fails at today's defaults: a 1.5 cm stripe at r 0.05 reflects 1.28x the PT's width; at r >= 0.15 it is gone (2 % of the PT's peak). AA off brings the raw back (x1.50 / x1.14) but not the denoised: the upscaler's clamp and the FFX denoiser drop sparse glossy hits on small emitters (X2 / X3 measure against it).
- SSR depth thickness turns thin geometry into a slab: a floating strip reflects 4x too wide (r 0.05) and 70x too bright (r 0.3); RT matches the PT. Scene `lineSpreadThin` for X1 #2.
- examples/ssr contact flickers at rest under the upscaler: consecutive frames 4.5/255 (AA off 0.09), cycling with the jitter; offscreen 1.6.
- Sponza: denoised glossy MAD worse than raw (43 vs 28.5). Grazing scene: denoised over-blurred (gradient x0.78, x0.52 at 60 deg); + RT 10-23/255 darker than the PT on the emissive wall (emissive on traced hits suspected).

**Experiment infra (W71 review, W71 / W72 notes, 2026-11-13)**
- The "default is identical" checks in `pnpm check` compare setting-absent vs setting-at-default in one build, so they can't catch drift from main (W71's RT branch moved defaults 14/255 by merely being compiled). Wire a main-build cross-check (W71's `docs/research/w71/scripts/xbuild*.sh`) into `pnpm check`, or a stored per-backend hash. Only Metal has been compared; D3D12 / Vulkan never.
- The bench's first configuration on a page reads differently (W72: door 115.7 vs 109.8) and X0's `lineSpread` returns empty on a page's first configuration (W71); streams use a throwaway warm-up config. Fix the warm-up in the bench.
- examples/ssr without `?manual` throws once: `TypeError: Cannot read properties of null (reading 'mipmaps')` in `packReflectionProbesTable` (ReflectionProbesNode.ts:76) (W73 saw it; metrics unaffected).
- Bench `ensure()` keeps keys a previous run set; pass explicit nulls to clear (W73).
- W74 (D5062): the half-rate fill costs ~1.7 ns per filled px (gather + scatter), so it only pays where a glossy traced ray is costly. A temporal pattern gentler than a per-frame checkerboard flip may cut the remaining +2-5 % orbit flicker at 0.05.
- W74 (review fix 7): fill px with no usable neighbour (3.9 % on contact) alternate traced / probe each frame as the checkerboard flips. Intersect could keep a quad at full rate where last frame's fill failed (a per-quad flag read in intersect).
- W74: half-rate raw signal still differs ±5-7 % per scene (contact -7.3 %, moving +5.5 %; denoised within 1.2 %): filled misses shift by up to a quad and take that pixel's probe term.
- W75: on the ssr page at full resolution, `halfRateReady` stayed false after 80 frames (W75 side note): verify a `tracedMissRate: 'half'` request actually starts `prepareHalfRate()` there (it needs `tracing`), or whether the page never asked.
- W72: the bench's Measure of ours-rt in the first config of a page (with ours-raw / ours-denoised measured alongside) reads differently from the same config re-run (carConcept w50 door 115.7 vs 109.8); lineSpread's first call on a page has no PT at r 0.05. Scripts add a discarded warm-up config.
- W72: with FFX's occupancy exit, consecutive frames at a fixed frameIndex differ in ~0.15 % px (the ray list's atomic order picks which rays share a subgroup); bit-for-bit comparisons need `minTraversalOccupancy: 0`.
- W72: the hit-colour pyramid costs 0.24-0.26 ms at 1080p (rgba16float, 8 levels incl. a full-res copy); start at half res or use rg11b10ufloat.
- W72: agents share the session scratchpad directory; another agent's server log overwrote `scratchpad/vite.log`. Use a per-agent subfolder.
- Flaky: hybrid's half-res + ratio + half-rate smoke line failed once right after a branch switch, then passed twice (W72); likely the async fill pipelines not ready (W75's halfRateReady note). Make the check wait for `halfRateReady`. Again on W80's merge check (5452, shared GPU): `half-rate skipped 0, filled 0`, then passed twice with 940 / 3594 (the same numbers as main).
- Flaky under GPU load: `ssr` 'rayReuse ... then changes the radiance' failed once on main after the W77 merge (ratio's async pipelines not built inside the check's frame window), passed on re-run. Make the check wait for the ratio pipelines to be ready (a readiness flag) instead of a frame count.
- Flaky under GPU load: after the W78 merge `--only hybrid` exited 2 once with no FAIL line (runner / browser exit, not an assertion), passed in full on re-run. Watch for a pattern.
- W76 (flicker, watch first): contact rest drift 0.78-0.82 at the new defaults vs W70's 0.71-0.73 (+7-12 %, at the 0.05 floor; orbit std -3 %), not separable from noise on the shared GPU. Re-measure on a quiet machine; if real, find which default (cone, compensation, relative thickness with RT) drives it.
- Within one page, contact under the upscaler varies more than "exact after a reset" (drift 0.745 vs 0.793, orbit 9.75 vs 9.44 for the same setting).

**From the external SSR sources (2026-11-12, DiligentFX / JBU / Stochastic SSR)**
- All nine ideas are approved as experiments and planned as streams X0-X5 in `docs/plans/ssr-sources-experiments.md` (settings with today's output as default, shared test scenes, decide by measurement; DIVERGES D5000-D5099). Waiting for the running flows to wrap up; the maintainer launches it. Research: `docs/research/ssr-external-sources.md`.

**pixi adapter (W18, W19: on pixi-3d)**
- Raw glossy SSR on the pixi3d arcade floor (r 0.06) is salt-and-pepper without the denoiser; with `FfxReflectionDenoiser` it's clean but soft. A pixi user without the Denoiser repo sees the raw signal.
- The arcade captures its probe once (the signs animate after); the probe has no box projection (open plaza) and sees black where the sky is (D2104), so probe-only reflections of the signs are parallax-shifted and stale.
- The G-buffer pass reads pixi-3d's material store by layout (D2101): a beta update that changes Material3D's packing breaks roughness / metalness silently; the arcade's G-buffer check (mirror + gold px) would catch it.
- The pixi composite's split-sum term is now core's separable table (W34, D2004), not measured against pixi-3d rendering its own environment specular: an env-only compare like Babylon's `iblCompare` (scene-colour bit off vs composite with SSR and probes off) would show what is left (prefilter, multi-scatter `FmsEms`).
- The dts build peaks at ~10 GB with pixi's types (heap raised to 12 GB); splitting the dts step per engine would shrink it but duplicates core's classes across entries.

**Performance**
- Denoised hybrid costs 0.9-1.4 ms over raw at 1080p. FFX runs full screen: feed it our denoiser tile list (needs the denoiser repo to take lists, D1400), and drop the second probe lookup / signal pass (D1401). mirrorWall is the scene at ms parity with three + denoise (ssr-gate.md, W10).
- Probe lookup 0.117 → 0.127 ms after the core move (noise level). Bake is CPU-encode bound at 128–256 px.
- Time the G-buffer, copies and composite in the three SSR adapter (W6).
- The RT pass sits at 8 storage buffers per stage (the default limit); pack buffers or request the limit before adding one (the guard throws clearly).

**Test robustness**
- Probe parity checks compare full lit images, so the flake above can fail them under load; compare atlas texels and the probe term alone (F3's plan, stopped).
- W7's scatter tolerance in the time-slice checks.
- The denoise seam's `roughnessUnpack` (W34, D1802) runs with identity only so far: Babylon has no denoiser hookup yet; check it on the GPU when it gets one.

## Agreed interfaces between streams

These are the seams the streams code against before the others land. Change one only by updating it here in the same commit.

### BC6H encoder (W2 → W1)

```ts
// packages/core/src/encode/bc6h.ts
export interface BC6HEncodeOptions { quality?: 'fast' | 'normal'; }
/** Encode every mip and layer of an rgba16float 2D-array (or cube) texture into a new bc6h-rgb-ufloat texture.
 *  Requires device.features.has('texture-compression-bc'). Mip extents below 4 px are padded to one block. */
export function createBC6HEncoder(device: GPUDevice, options?: { maxScratchBytes?: number }): { // F1: scratch chunked to the device limits
  encode(src: GPUTexture, opts?: BC6HEncodeOptions): GPUTexture; // records into its own command encoder and submits
  encodeInto(cmd: GPUCommandEncoder, src: GPUTexture, dst: GPUTexture, opts?: BC6HEncodeOptions): void;
  destroy(): void;
};
```

Until W2 lands, W1 leaves the `bc6h` column of the format axis as "pending encoder" and measures everything else.

### Probe layout functions (W1, consumed later by phase 2)

```ts
// packages/core/src/probes/layouts/types.ts
export type ProbeLayoutId = 'cube2d-warp' | 'cube2d-none' | 'cube-hw' | 'octa' | 'octa-hemi';
export interface ProbeLayout {
  id: ProbeLayoutId;
  /** WGSL defining dirForTexel(texel: vec2u, layer: u32, mip: u32, size: u32) -> vec3f
   *  and sampleLayout(tex, samp, probe: u32, dir: vec3f, lod: f32) -> vec4f for this layout. */
  wgsl: string;
  /** Texture shape for N probes at `size` px (mip 0 face or octa side). */
  textureDescriptor(size: number, probes: number, format: GPUTextureFormat): GPUTextureDescriptor;
  viewDimension: GPUTextureViewDimension;
  // added by W1 (as built):
  textureType: string;      // WGSL type sampleLayout takes, e.g. 'texture_cube_array<f32>'
  layersPerProbe: number;   // 6 (cube layouts) or 1 (octa)
  mipLevelCount: number;    // maxLod + 1; mip l holds lodToRoughness(l, maxLod)
}
// createProbeLayout(id, { maxLod }); `size` in dirForTexel is the mip-0 size. Directions are in
// texture space (WebGPU cube face rules, no mirroring); three's adapter mirrors x first.
// LayoutPrefilter(device, layout, size, sourceSize): r187 plan through dirForTexel, bit-identical to
// ProbePrefilter for cube2d-*. ProbeFormatWriter: rgba16float / rg11b10ufloat / rgb9e5ufloat / bc6h.
// W35 (D805): every layout's own WGSL is prefixed (`layout.prefix`: pl_cube2d_warp_, pl_cube2d_none_,
// pl_cube_hw_, pl_octa<maxLod>_) and comes in named chunks (`layout.chunks`, emit each key once;
// `probeLayoutsWgsl([...])`); call `layout.sampleFunction` / `layout.dirFunction` to mix layouts in one
// module. `layout.wgsl` keeps `sampleLayout` / `dirForTexel` as aliases for single-layout modules.
// LayoutPrefilter(..., { cubeSourceCopy }) (default on for compatibility-mode devices, D4100).
```

### Radiance fallback (SSSR miss → probes/env), used by W3 now, filled by phase 2

A WGSL snippet plus a bind group, like the trace seam:

```wgsl
fn sampleRadianceFallback(P: vec3f, dir: vec3f, roughness: f32) -> vec3f
```

W3 ships a default implementation (a single `texture_cube<f32>` environment, or a constant colour) and takes any other snippet + bind group layout at pipeline creation. As built (W3): `P` and `dir` are world space; `roughness` is **perceptual**: `sqrt(alpha)` along the mirror direction for reflective pixels above the roughness threshold and, by default, for screen-space misses too (each pixel its own lookup, lerped by hit confidence; W30, D3100); with `settings.fallbackRoughness: 'mirror'`, misses take FFX's roughness 0 along the sampled direction. Traced misses (`core/src/trace/`) still use roughness 0 (D900). The snippet's bindings live in `@group(1)`; pass its layout as `radianceFallback.layout` and the bind group as `inputs.fallbackBindGroup`. It may read the tracer's uniform block `P` (`P.iblFactor`, `P.fallbackColor`).

Filled by W7 (phase 2): `probeRadianceFallback(set: ProbeSet)` returns `{ wgsl, layout, bindGroup }`. Its `sampleRadianceFallback` is `probeRadianceWithEnvironment(P, dir, roughness) * P.iblFactor` over the set's lookup module at `@group(1)` (atlas, sampler, table: 1 storage buffer). The same object plugs into `createScreenSpaceReflections({ radianceFallback })` + `inputs.fallbackBindGroup` and `createTracedReflections({ radianceFallback })` + `fallbackBindGroup` (traced misses, and `fallbackHitShading`'s probes at the hit along N at roughness 1). The same set can also be the pass's hit shading (`hitSpecularShading` / `hitShading: probeHitShading(device, set)`, group 3): that copy's names carry `_g3`, and the passes keep the lookup's shared includes once (`dedupeWgslChunks` over `markedChunkWgsl` chunks; W64, D4901). A seam that concatenates the lookup's WGSL into a module built elsewhere should run the module through `dedupeWgslChunks` too.

### Probe core (W7, phase 2)

```ts
// packages/core/src/probes/set.ts
const set = new ProbeSet(device, { capacity, layout?: 'cube-hw', format?: 'rgba16float' | 'rgb9e5ufloat', sourceSize?: 256,   // W31: 256 (D23)
  mirrorX?, dominantDirection?, refit?, frameIrradianceWgsl?, lodMapping?: 'r187' | 'alpha',   // W31: lodMapping default 'alpha' (D101); refit defaults on with frameIrradianceWgsl (D803)
  distanceRoughness?, roughnessLerp? });   // W26 (D53): Lagarde's seam options, also on ProbeLookupOptions (core default off)
set.capture(encoder, slot, cube); set.captureEnvironment(encoder, cube);   // cube: sourceSize px faces, mip 0 read
set.setVolumes([{ slot, center, halfSize, fade?, projectionSize?, projectionOffset?, origin?, boxProjection?, priority? }]);
set.update();                         // uploads header + volumes; computeIrradiance(encoder) writes the L1 SH rows
set.wgsl({ group })                   // probeRadiance(P, R, r) -> vec4f (radiance / coverage, coverage),
                                      // probeRadianceWithEnvironment(P, R, r) -> vec3f, ...N(P, N, R, r) variants,
                                      // probeSpecularOcclusion(NdotV, ao, r) -> f32; bindings 0..2 = set.bindGroupLayout
// engines that own their bindings: probeLookupIncludesWgsl(o) once per module + probeLookupFunctionWgsl(o, name):
// fn name(atlas, samp, table: ptr<storage, array<vec4f>, read>, P, N, R, roughness, envRotation: mat4x4f, frameIrr, withEnv) -> vec4f   (F2: was envR; the env slot now takes the bent direction)
// table layout: packages/core/src/probes/table.ts (packProbeTable, rows of vec4f)
// W35: several sets of any layouts in one module: probeLookupWgsl([a.lookupOptions({ group: 1 }), b.lookupOptions({ group: 2, suffix: 'B' })])
//   -> probeRadiance(...) samples a, probeRadianceB(...) samples b (D805); probeLookupIncludesWgsl([...]) / probeLookupIncludeChunks([...])
//   take several option sets (the include no longer depends on mirrorX or any option, only the layouts).
// W35: captureTransform?: { axes?: ['-x','y','z'] ..., faces?: [{ layer, flipX?, flipY? } x 6] } on ProbeSet, the lookup options and
//   ProbeIrradiance (core probes/capture.ts, D1805): axes at lookup (CAPTURE_MIRROR_X = mirrorX, CAPTURE_MIRROR_Y = Babylon's cube as is),
//   faces as ProbeSet.capture() reads the capture in (CAPTURE_BABYLON_FACES = D1805's pass, CAPTURE_CAMERA_FACES = pixi-3d's images).
//   `mirrorX: true` stays as its alias (both at once throws).
// W35 (D2204): ProbeIrradiance.encode(encoder, atlasTexture, ...) views the atlas as the layout's view dimension; ProbeSet's default
//   layout is cube2d-warp on a compatibility-mode device (no cube-array views there).
```

### Frame contract inputs (W4 → W3's three adapter, later)

W3 develops on synthetic, analytic inputs written by compute (exact depth/normal for a plane and boxes), so it never waits on W4. W4 produces the same textures from a three scene, matching SPEC's frame-contract table. The three SSSR node joins the two after both merge.

W4's side, as landed:

```ts
// packages/core/src/frame/contract.ts (exported from @pmndrs/reflections/core)
interface FrameInputs { width; height; textures: FrameTextures; conventions: FrameConventions; camera: FrameCamera; frameIndex }
interface FrameTextures { depth; normal; roughness: { texture, channel }; metalness: { texture, channel }; albedo; motion; sceneColor; lit? } // GPUTexture handles
interface FrameConventions { depthInverted; depthLinear; normalUnpackMul; normalUnpackAdd; roughnessIsPerceptual; roughnessUnpack?: { mul, add } /* W34, default identity */; motionVectorScale: [x, y]; sceneColorSpecular: 'none' | 'direct' }
// FrameCamera: denoiser-core's FrameCamera fields (view, projection, prevView, prevProjection, jitter, near, far, position, reversedDepth) + prevViewProjection
//   + projectionJittered? (F1: `projection` is unjittered when the adapter is given it, D405)

// packages/three/src/frame/FrameGBuffer.ts (exported from @pmndrs/reflections/three)
const gbuffer = new FrameGBuffer(renderer, { lit?: boolean | 'auto', motion?: 'uv' | 'ndc', width?, height? });
const frame: FrameInputs = gbuffer.render(scene, camera, { jitter?, projection? /* unjittered */ }); // each call = one frame of history
gbuffer.resetHistory(); // next render: prev = current, motion 0
gbuffer.textures.normalRoughness; // the three textures, for TSL sampling
```

Layout from three: depth32float; normal = rgba16float world normal (mul 1, add 0) with perceptual roughness in .a; albedo = rgba8unorm base colour with metalness in .a; motion = rg16float uv-space, current − previous, `motionVectorScale` [1, 1] (MRT slot and texture named `velocity`, which three needs for instanced/skinned/batched history, D700); sceneColor = rgba16float emissive + direct + indirect diffuse (no specular). Three-only, with `lit` when the device allows 46 B/sample (W27, `specular: 'auto'`): `textures.specular` rgba16float (rgb = three's env-specular single scattering, a = its specular occlusion x env intensity) and `textures.shadingRoughness` r16float (three's geometry-adjusted roughness); not in `FrameTextures`, read by the three composite only.

### Trace seam (W8 → @pmndrs/bvh, W7)

```ts
// packages/core/src/trace/provider.ts (exported from @pmndrs/reflections/core)
interface BindingSlot { group: number; firstBinding: number }          // = ddgi's BindingSlot
interface TraceProvider {
  name: string;
  code(slot: BindingSlot): string;   // bindings at slot, in `entries` order; defines traceFirstHit (+ traceHitMaterial if capabilities.hitMaterial)
  entries: readonly ModuleBindingEntry[]; resources(): readonly GPUBindingResource[]; version: number; storageBufferCount: number;
  capabilities: { hitNormal: boolean; hitMaterial: boolean; anyHit?: boolean; shadingNormal?: boolean };  // anyHit: W25, shadingNormal: W28
  layoutVersion?: number;            // @pmndrs/bvh: GPU_LAYOUT_VERSION, checked by the provider at start-up
}
```

```wgsl
// declared by the pass (HIT_WGSL) before the provider's code
struct Hit { t: f32 /* < 0: miss */, triangle: u32, barycentrics: vec2f /* weights of corners 1, 2 */, material: u32, instance: u32, normal: vec3f /* world, 0 if !hitNormal */, frontFace: bool }
struct HitMaterial { albedo: vec3f, emissive: vec3f }
fn traceFirstHit(origin: vec3f, dir: vec3f, tMax: f32) -> Hit                 // provider
fn traceHitMaterial(hit: Hit) -> HitMaterial                                  // provider, or the pass's grey default
fn traceAny(origin: vec3f, dir: vec3f, tMax: f32) -> bool                     // provider if capabilities.anyHit, else the pass's (traceFirstHit) (W25)
fn traceHitShadingNormal(hit: Hit) -> vec3f                                   // provider if capabilities.shadingNormal (vertex normals at hit.triangle x barycentrics, world, any length/side; 0 = none), else 0 (W28, D1700)
fn traceLightVisible(P: vec3f, N: vec3f, L: vec3f, tMax: f32) -> bool         // pass: shadow ray, origin offset as D8; true with hitShadows off (W25)
fn shadeTracedHit(hit: Hit, P: vec3f, N: vec3f, dir: vec3f) -> vec3f          // HitShading (pass option); may call sampleRadianceFallback. N: the shading normal if any, on the ray's side
```

The traced pass binds group 0 (itself: 3 storage buffers, 4 storage textures), 1 (radiance fallback, same snippet as SSSR), 2 (provider), 3 (hit shading: `environmentHitShading`, or W28's `probeHitShading(device, set | lookup options)` = lookup atlas / sampler / table at 0..2 + light uniform at 3 + a multi-size set's atlases 1.. at 4.. (W47, D4702), 1 storage buffer; three picks it when `scene.environmentNode` is `reflectionProbes(probes)`). With 3 storage buffers of its own, a provider may use 5 on a default device, 4 with the probe fallback (its table is one more). F2: `createTracedReflections` counts all four groups (storage buffers and storage textures; `RadianceFallback` / `HitShading` declare theirs with `storageBufferCount` / `storageTextureCount`) against `device.limits` and throws with the limit to raise.

**Plugged in (W14, on bvh `main` since W48):** `PmndrsBvhTraceProvider` in `packages/core/src/trace/pmndrsBvh.ts` is that wrapper; the three items below landed first on bvh's `reflections-integration` branch and are now on its `main` (layout 2: `bvhTraceClosest(BvhRay) -> BvhHit` with `normal` and per-instance `material`, `GPU_LAYOUT_VERSION` 2). What follows is the pre-W14 note, in the branch's `gi*` names.

- Full-suite run: babylon `?probeFace` 'page not ready' once after ~270 checks in one Chrome (passes alone); the first full run timed out in quickstart 'rt + preset performance' (passes alone).
- W89 / W91: r3f 'rt + performance' rest-flicker check sits on its 1.5 % bound (1.43-1.55 % over nine runs, before and after); webpack / Next.js with the optional `@pmndrs/raytracer` import untested; bench uses pose 'hero', which Sponza lacks (ours luma 73.9 vs reference ~30); core probe fallback + probe hit shading binds 9 storage buffers (sharing the probe table binding would make it 8). `docs/research/w89/README.md`.

**For @pmndrs/bvh to plug in:** a ~40-line wrapper whose `code(slot)` is `traceModuleWgsl(slot)` (+ its dependencies) followed by `traceFirstHit` calling `giTraceClosest(GiRay(origin, 0.0, dir, tMax))` and mapping `GiHit` (t, triangle, barycentrics, frontFace map one to one). What it lacks today: (1) a hit normal (GiHit has none; the walk has the triangle's corners and the instance transform, so a `giHitNormal(hit)` helper or a `normal` field would do; without it `hitNormal: false` and the pass shades with -dir); (2) an instance → material table (an extra binding, or a user word in the instance record); (3) the exported `GPU_LAYOUT_VERSION` constant (the split doc plans it; not in the source yet). Its 3 storage buffers fit (6 of 8 with the pass).
